Result: 方差最大化类获取函数将采样集中于角点，而负积分后验方差和期望预测信息增益则将采样向内移至沿坐标轴的内部壳层。这些模式与目标函数无关，获取行为可能由核几何主导。

Conclusion: 边界效应是核几何的固有属性，会导致获取行为偏离任务所需的探索目标，所提诊断工具可用于任意获取函数、核和有限域几何的偏差量化。

Abstract: Gaussian processes with stationary kernels on bounded domains exhibit inflated posterior variance near the boundary. Despite being a long-recognized artifact in geostatistics and a source of over-exploration in Bayesian optimization, the causes and effects of boundary-induced acquisition bias are underexplored. We trace the root cause to a simple geometric mechanism: the truncation of the kernel correlation neighborhood at the domain boundary creates an observation-independent distortion that worsens with dimensionality. We show how this distortion manifests across three acquisition classes: variance maximization concentrates selections at the corners, whereas negative integrated posterior variance and expected predictive information gain move selections inward to axis-aligned interior shells. These patterns arise without reference to any objective function, meaning that acquisition behavior can be dominated by kernel geometry rather than the desired task-specific uncertainty. To quantify this, we introduce a function-free selection-profile diagnostic for arbitrary acquisitions, kernels, and bounded-domain geometries.

</details>


### [84] [Enabling KV Caching of Shared Prefix for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07571)
*Younghun Go,Jaehoon Han,Changyong Shin,Chuk Yoo,Gyeongsik Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出bicache，首个针对扩散语言模型（DLM）共享前缀的KV缓存技术。研究发现DLM中双向注意力导致传统LLM的KV缓存方法失效（准确率崩塌至近零）。bicache通过动态识别可安全复用共享前缀KV的浅层深度，在避免精度坍塌（仅0-1.8%差异）的同时，提升吞吐量36.3%-98.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有为LLM设计的KV缓存技术假设KV一旦计算即保持不变，但DLM的双向注意力机制下，更新任意token会动态改变整个上下文及其对应KV，导致共享前缀KV被污染，模型准确率几乎归零。需要为DLM设计新的共享前缀KV缓存技术以实现高吞吐服务。

Method: 基于关键观察：共享前缀KV在浅层保持稳定可复用，而浅层深度取决于每个请求中共享前缀token的比例。bicache通过动态识别可安全复用共享前缀KV的层深度，消除冗余计算。

Result: 相比现有技术，bicache将服务吞吐量提升36.3%-98.3%，且精度几乎无损失（仅0-1.8%差异）。

Conclusion: bicache是首个针对DLM共享前缀的高效KV缓存方案，解决了双向注意力下缓存失效的关键难题，证明了DLM浅层KV的稳定性和可复用性，为高吞吐DLM服务提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Key-value (KV) caching for shared prefixes is essential for high-throughput large language model (LLM) serving, but it faces critical challenges in emerging diffusion language models (DLMs). In DLMs, bidirectional attention means that updating any token dynamically alters the entire context and its corresponding KVs. Thus, existing caching techniques developed for LLMs, which assume that KVs remain invariant once computed, corrupt the shared prefix KVs. Our experiments show that applying these techniques to DLMs causes model accuracy to collapse to near zero.
  To unlock high-throughput DLM serving, we propose bidirectional prefix caching, bicache, the first KV caching technique for shared prefixes in DLMs. bicache is designed based on key observations from our comprehensive analysis: shared prefix KVs remain stable and reusable in shallow layers, while the depth of shallow layers depends on the fraction of shared prefix tokens in each request. Thus, bicache dynamically identifies a safe layer depth for reusing shared prefix KVs and eliminates redundant computation. Evaluations demonstrate that bicache significantly improves serving throughput by 36.3%-98.3% compared to existing techniques without accuracy collapse (only 0-1.8% difference).

</details>


### [85] [Mesh Graph Neural Network Framework for Accelerating Finite Element Simulation for Arbitrary Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08287)
*Josiah D. Kunz,Kamal Choudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种网格图网络（MGN）用于预测二维结构件的 von Mises 应力场，该模型通过编码节点类型、相对边特征和全局特征实现平移和旋转不变性，能够在不同几何形状下泛化而无需重新训练，在未见几何和载荷条件下 R² ≥ 0.97，显著优于传统机器学习模型（R² ≈ 0.01-0.86）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限元分析（FEA）在结构设计中至关重要，但计算成本高昂，尤其在评估多种设计迭代或载荷场景时。现有机器学习代理模型难以泛化到不同几何形状，该工作旨在解决这一关键局限性。

Method: 构建网格图网络（MGN），使用节点类型（固定边界、自由表面、孔边）、相对边特征（邻点间距离）和全局特征（施加载荷）作为输入，而非绝对节点坐标。该架构具有平移和旋转不变性。在11种板几何形状、20种载荷条件下训练，在7种未见几何和3种未见载荷上评估。

Result: 最佳情况下，MGN 在未见几何和未见载荷上 R² ≥ 0.97，传统模型（随机森林、梯度提升、K近邻）仅 R² ≈ 0.01-0.86。即使在较不理想的情况下，MGN 仍优于传统模型。

Conclusion: 该工作将 Pfaff 等人的网格基仿真框架扩展到结构力学，证明了图神经网络可作为跨几何形状的有限元分析的高效代理模型。

Abstract: Finite element analysis (FEA) is essential for structural design but remains computationally expensive, particularly when evaluating multiple design iterations or load scenarios. Machine learning surrogate models offer a promising alternative, yet most approaches struggle with a critical limitation: generalizing across varying geometries. This work presents a mesh graph network (MGN) for predicting von Mises stress fields in 2D structural components with arbitrary hole geometries. Unlike traditional machine learning approaches that use absolute node coordinates as features, the proposed model builds on existing MGN frameworks that encode node types (e.g., fixed boundary, free surface, hole edge), relative edge features (distance between neighbors), and global features (applied load). This architecture is inherently translation- and rotation-invariant, enabling generalization to unseen geometries without retraining. The MGN was trained on 11 plate geometries under 20 load conditions and evaluated on 7 unseen geometries and 3 unseen loads. In the most favorable case, the model achieves $R^2 \geq 0.97$ on an unseen geometry and unseen load, compared to $R^2 \approx 0.01$--$0.86$ for conventional models (Random Forest, Gradient Boosting , K-Nearest Neighbors) trained on identical data. However, even in less favorable cases, the MGN model still outperforms conventional models. This work extends the mesh-based simulation framework of Pfaff et al. (arXiv:2010.03409) to structural mechanics, demonstrating that graph neural networks can serve as efficient surrogates for finite element analysis across varying geometries.

</details>


### [86] [Outage Detection in Self-Healing Smart Grids Using Reinforcement Learning with Spectral Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07583)
*Lihui Liu,Mucun Sun,Caisheng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于谱图强化学习的配电网故障管理框架，利用谱图神经网络学习最优供电恢复策略，在三个IEEE测试系统上实现了接近最优的实时性能和良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习方法响应慢、计算成本高，不适合智能电网的故障缓解；现有基于GNN的方法在空间域操作，无法捕捉频域中重要的全局结构模式和系统级交互信息。

Method: 提出谱图强化学习框架，使用谱图神经网络（Spectral GNN）学习配电网的最优供电恢复策略，在频域中建模电力网络的全局结构模式。

Result: 在IEEE 13-bus、34-bus和123-bus三个改进测试系统上，该方法实现了接近最优的实时性能，并在多种故障场景下具有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 谱图强化学习框架能够有效提升配电网的故障恢复能力，在实时性和泛化性方面均优于传统方法。

Abstract: Self-healing smart grids can quickly adjust their network configuration during outages to minimize power disruptions. During an outage, several actions can be taken, such as network reconfiguration through switching operations and emergency load shedding. However, traditional machine learning methods for outage mitigation are not well suited for smart grids due to their slow response time and high computational cost. To address these challenges, recent studies have explored reinforcement learning to automatically perform network reconfiguration. In these approaches, the control policy is typically modeled using a graph neural network (GNN). However, conventional GNNs operate in the spatial domain and may fail to capture important relationships in the frequency domain. Frequency-domain information is particularly useful for modeling global structural patterns and system-wide interactions in power networks. In this paper, we propose a spectral graph reinforcement learning framework for outage management in distribution networks to enhance system resilience. Our model learns the optimal power restoration policy using a spectral graph neural network. We evaluate the proposed method on three modified IEEE test systems: the 13-bus, 34-bus, and 123-bus networks. Experimental results show that our approach achieves near-optimal performance in real time and generalizes well across a wide range of outage scenarios.

</details>


### [87] [From Human Guidance to Autonomy: Agent Skill System for End-to-End LLM Deployment on Spatial NPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07586)
*Jiajie Li,Erwei Wang,Zhiru Zhang,Samuel Bayliss*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段方法论，在AMD XDNA 2 NPU上实现了从人类引导到智能体自主的端到端LLM部署流程。第一阶段通过人类引导的智能体辅助部署Llama-3.2-1B，相比手工优化基线实现了2.2倍prefill和4.0倍decode加速；第二阶段将经验蒸馏为8阶段智能体技能系统，自主部署了8个额外的解码器LLM，每个部署在0.5-4小时内完成且几乎无需人工干预，其中3个模型性能达到或超过参考部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间NPU为边缘LLM推理提供能效平台，但端到端部署LLM到资源受限的空间NPU上仍然劳动密集。现有的AI编码智能体研究主要集中在单核优化而非完整的端到端部署，需要一种自动化方法来降低部署成本。

Method: 两阶段方法论：第一阶段，通过人类引导的智能体辅助开发Llama-3.2-1B的参考部署，记录优化轨迹和教训作为结构化文档；第二阶段，将文档蒸馏为包含8个阶段的智能体技能系统，每个阶段严格强制执行数值正确性，实现从人类引导到智能体自主的过渡。

Result: Llama-3.2-1B参考部署实现2.2倍prefill和4.0倍decode加速；8个额外LLM（包括Llama-3.2-3B, SmolLM2-1.7B, Qwen2.5系列, Qwen3系列）成功端到端部署，完成时间0.5-4小时，几乎无需人工指导；其中3个模型性能达到或超过参考部署。

Conclusion: 所提出的智能体技能系统能够将人类专家的优化知识有效迁移到自动化部署流程中，实现了对未见LLM的功能泛化，且自动生成的实现具有与人工优化相当的竞争力，大幅降低了空间NPU上LLM端到端部署的人力成本。

Abstract: Spatial neural processing units (NPUs) provide an energy-efficient platform for edge LLM inference, but efficiently deploying an LLM end-to-end on such hardware remains labor-intensive. Although AI coding agents have begun to lower this cost, existing studies have largely focused on single-kernel optimization rather than end-to-end LLM deployment on resource-constrained spatial NPUs.
  We present a two-stage methodology, instantiated on the AMD XDNA 2 NPU, that progresses from human-guided development to agent autonomy. In the first stage, we develop a reference deployment of Llama-3.2-1B through human-guided agent assistance. The resulting implementation achieves a speedup of 2.2x on prefill and 4.0x on decode over the hand-optimized baseline, with the optimization trajectory and its lessons recorded as structured documentation throughout. In the second stage, we distill the documentation into an agent skill system consisting of eight phases, orchestrating the optimization and debugging skill sets, with numerical correctness strictly enforced at each phase.
  Using our agent skill system, we autonomously deploy eight additional decoder-only LLMs (Llama-3.2-3B, SmolLM2-1.7B, Qwen2.5-{0.5B, 1.5B, 3B}, Qwen3-{0.6B, 1.7B, 4B}) end-to-end on the AMD XDNA 2 NPU using the open-source compiler stack. To our knowledge, these models have not previously been deployed on AMD NPUs via any open-source software stack. Each deployment completes in 0.5-4 hours of agent wall time with almost no human guidance, and passes the numerical-correctness gates, demonstrating functional generalization to previously unencountered LLMs. Three of the eight match or exceed the sustained performance of our Llama-3.2-1B reference deployment, suggesting that the resulting implementations can be competitive without additional model-specific human engineering.

</details>


### [88] [The Routing Plateau: Understanding and Breaking the Accuracy Limits of LLM Routers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07587)
*Yifan Lu,Qiyue Zhang,Shenrun Zhang,Zhibo Yu,Zhuang Wang,Hanjie Chen,Jiarong Xing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对21种LLM路由方法进行了大规模研究，发现大多数方法存在"路由平台期"现象：各方法准确率相似且远低于理论最优路由。平台期主要由可预测性瓶颈导致——路由器主要学习全局平均性能趋势而非细粒度的查询特定信号，因此能解决重叠的简单问题但集体在需要实例级路由决策的困难问题上失败。更大训练集、更强编码器和端到端微调可进一步突破平台期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升LLM服务的成本-质量权衡，通过动态路由选择最优模型响应每个查询，但现有路由方法性能存在共同的局限性尚未被系统揭示。

Method: 对21种路由方法在5个基准上进行大规模比较研究，包括基于聚类、学习分类器、成对排序和置信度方法；分析性能平台期的成因（可预测性瓶颈）；探索突破平台期的策略（更大数据集、更强编码器、端到端微调）。

Result: 发现路由平台期现象：多数方法（包括kNN）准确率相近且收敛于狭窄性能区间，远低于oracle。平台期主要由可预测性瓶颈导致，路由器学习的是全局平均趋势而非实例级信号。增加训练数据、增强编码器、端到端微调可提升路由精度。

Conclusion: 当前路由方法的共同限制在于缺乏细粒度查询特定路由信号，研究成果为构建更有效的路由系统提供了见解和可行方向。

Abstract: LLM routing has become a popular approach to improve the cost-quality trade-off of LLM services by dynamically selecting a model for each query. Recent work has explored a broad range of routing methods, including clustering-based routers, learned classifiers, pairwise ranking, and confidence-based approaches. Our extensive study of 21 routing methods across five benchmarks reveals a consistent phenomenon that we call the routing plateau: many methods, including kNN, achieve very similar accuracy and converge to a narrow performance range that remains far below the oracle router. Our investigation shows that the plateau is largely caused by a predictability bottleneck: current routers mainly learn global averaged model-performance trends rather than fine-grained query-specific routing signals. As a result, they solve overlapping easy queries but collectively fail on hard queries that require instance-specific routing decisions. We further study how to move beyond the plateau and find that larger training datasets, stronger encoders, and end-to-end fine-tuning can further improve routing accuracy. These findings characterize the common limits of current routing methods and provide insights and actionable directions for the community to build more effective routing systems.

</details>


### [89] [ResearchClawBench: A Benchmark for End-to-End Autonomous Scientific Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07591)
*Wanghan Xu,Shuo Li,Tianlin Ye,Qinglong Cao,Yixin Chen,Hengjian Gao,Yiheng Wang,Qi Li,Kun Li,Sheng Xu,Shengdu Chai,Fangchen Yu,Xiangyu Zhao,Zhangrui Zhao,Weijie Ma,Zijie Guo,Haoyu Zhou,Haoxiang Yin,Lixue Cheng,Chaofan Hu,Haoxuan Li,Lu Mi,Xuxuan Xie,Yifan Zhou,Ruizhe Chen,Zhiwang Zhou,Xingjian Guo,Yuhao Zhou,Xuming He,Shengyuan Xu,Xinyu Gu,Jiamin Wu,Mianxin Liu,Chunfeng Song,Fenghua Ling,Dongzhan Zhou,Shixiang Tang,Yuqiang Li,Mao Su,Peng Ye,Siqi Sun,Bin Wang,Xue Yang,Zhenfei Yin,Tianfan Fu,Guangtao Zhai,Wanli Ouyang,Bo Zhang,Lei Bai,Wenlong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ResearchClawBench是一个跨10个科学领域、包含40个任务的自主科学研究基准。每个任务基于已发表论文，提供相关文献和原始数据，隐藏目标论文。专家策划的多模态评分标准将目标科学成果分解为加权指标。评估了7个自主研究代理和17个原生LLM，最强代理Claude Code平均21.5分，最强LLM Claude-Opus-4.7平均20.7分，LLM前沿均值仅26.5分。错误分析显示失败集中在实验方案不匹配、证据不匹配和缺少科学核心。该基准为衡量自主科学研究进展提供了可复现的评估前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码代理越来越多地用于科学工作，但其端到端的自主研究能力难以验证，现有基准缺乏对完整科学研究流程（从文献到数据到成果）的评估。作者希望构建一个可复现的基准来衡量自主科学研究进展。

Method: 构建包含40个任务、10个科学领域的基准ResearchClawBench。每个任务基于真实已发表论文，提供相关文献和原始数据，隐藏目标论文。使用专家策划的多模态评分标准将目标科学成果分解为加权标准。提出统一评估协议，通过ResearchHarness轻量级框架评估7个自主研究代理和17个原生LLM。

Result: 最强自主代理Claude Code平均21.5分，最强ResearchHarness LLM Claude-Opus-4.7平均20.7分，LLM前沿均值仅26.5分。错误集中在实验方案不匹配、证据不匹配和缺少科学核心。

Conclusion: 当前系统远未达到可靠的重新发现能力，ResearchClawBench为衡量自主科学研究进展提供了可复现的评估基准。

Abstract: AI coding agents are increasingly used for scientific work, but their end-to-end autonomous research capability remains difficult to verify. We present ResearchClawBench, a benchmark for evaluating autonomous scientific research across 40 tasks from 10 scientific domains. Each task is grounded in a real published paper, provides related literature and raw data, and hides the target paper during evaluation. Expert-curated multimodal rubrics decompose the target scientific artifacts into weighted criteria, enabling evaluation of target-paper-level re-discovery while leaving room for new discovery. We evaluate seven autonomous research (auto-research) agents under a unified protocol and seventeen native LLMs through the lightweight ResearchHarness. Current systems remain far from reliable re-discovery: the strongest autonomous agent, Claude Code, averages 21.5, and the strongest ResearchHarness LLM, Claude-Opus-4.7, averages 20.7, with an LLM frontier mean of only 26.5. Error analysis shows that failures concentrate in experimental protocol mismatch, evidence mismatch, and missing scientific core. ResearchClawBench provides a reproducible evaluation frontier for measuring progress toward autonomous scientific research.

</details>


### [90] [A Topological Characterization of Graph Neural Networks via Stochastic Block Model Embeddings on the n-Sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07598)
*Gopal Anantharaman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种拓扑框架，通过将图神经网络（GNN）在随机块模型（SBM）上诱导的结构映射到单位球面上，生成低维的、与问题无关的"指纹"，用于GNN模型的可视化比较和迁移学习候选检索。该框架基于图空间紧致性、Frieze-Kannan弱正则引理及MPNN关于割距离的Lipschitz连续性三个经典支柱，并讨论了高维测度集中现象的障碍，最后提出了五个未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN模型评估和比较缺乏统一、普适的拓扑框架。训练后的GNN模型通常作为黑箱存在，难以进行结构化的跨模型比较或检索，阻碍了迁移学习中候选模型的筛选效率。

Method: 将随机块模型（SBM）在图信号空间上诱导的结构映射到单位n维球面上，利用割距离图空间的紧致性、Frieze-Kannan弱正则引理及其图信号扩展、以及MPNN关于割距离的Lipschitz连续性，构造显式的保测度映射，将SBM区域置于不相交的球冠上。

Result: 对于任意给定容忍度ε>0，足够大图上训练的MPNN可因子化（误差在ε内）通过一个有界复杂度的阶梯图信号；构造的映射使SBM区域分布在不相交球冠上，生成可可视化、问题无关的低维"指纹"，支持模型动物园中的近邻搜索和迁移学习候选检索。

Conclusion: 该拓扑框架为GNN比较提供了一种新范式，同时指出了高维测度集中带来的挑战。未来方向包括双曲/格拉斯曼流形替代、Gromov-Wasserstein距离、信息几何SBM流形、持续同调的层嵌入指纹以及基于图信号谱分解的光谱距离基线。

Abstract: We propose a topological framework for comparing trained Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) by mapping the Stochastic Block Models (SBMs) induced on the graphon-signal space of a Message Passing Neural Network (MPNN) onto the unit $n$-sphere $\sphere^{n-1}\subset\R^n$. The construction rests on three classical pillars: the \emph{compactness} of the cut-distance graphon space $(\Wo,\cutdist)$ \citep{lovasz2006limits,lovasz2012large}, the Frieze--Kannan \emph{weak regularity lemma} together with its graphon-signal extension due to \citet{levie2023graphon}, and the Lipschitz continuity of MPNNs with respect to the cut-distance. We show that, for any prescribed tolerance $\varepsilon>0$, a trained MPNN $Φ$ acting on a sufficiently large graph factors (up to $\varepsilon$) through a step-graphon-signal of bounded complexity, and we construct an explicit measure-preserving map $Ψ_n\colon[0,1]\to\sphere^{n-1}$ that places the SBM regions on disjoint spherical caps. This produces a problem-agnostic, low-dimensional ``fingerprint'' of a trained GNN that is amenable to visual inspection and to nearest-neighbour search across model zoos, enabling \emph{transfer-learning candidate retrieval} without retraining. We discuss the obstruction posed by concentration of measure in high dimension -- a phenomenon directly relevant to LLM-scale embeddings. We close with five concrete future research directions: hyperbolic and Grassmannian alternatives to the spherical model, Gromov--Wasserstein distances on graphon-signals as an isometry-free alternative to the $n$-sphere map, an information-geometric (Fisher) reformulation of the SBM manifold, persistent-homology fingerprints of layer-wise embedding clouds, and a spectral-distance baseline derived from the graphon eigendecomposition.

</details>


### [91] [Sample-Efficient Post-Training for LEGO Spatial-Physics Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07602)
*Yuhuan Yuan,Zhouliang Yu,Minghao Liu,Weiyang Liu,Ge Lin Kan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM在乐高积木生成中存在一种数据导致的失效模式PhysHack（满足物理约束但几何错位、语义不一致或校准不良），并提出基于模型的数据选择方法仅用少量训练数据即可改善生成质量，同时引入PVPO强化学习方法，通过体素空间几何奖励耦合物理可行性，实验证明该方法能有效提升结构对齐、语义一致性、物理有效性和校准质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-based LEGO组装生成方法虽关注物理可行性，但仅满足物理约束并不足以保证可靠的物理推理——模型可能生成物理有效但几何错位、语义不一致或校准不良的结构。作者旨在识别并解决这一数据导致的失效模式PhysHack。

Method: 提出基于模型的数据选择方法，仅使用少量训练数据；在此基础上提出PVPO（样本高效的强化学习方法），将物理可行性与体素空间几何奖励相结合。

Result: PVPO在多种模型骨干和测试时缩放设置下均能提升结构对齐、语义一致性、物理有效性、结构稳定性和校准质量，同时减少对大量事后拒绝采样的依赖。校准实验表明PVPO通过使测试时选择更能预测语义和结构质量来缓解PhysHack。

Conclusion: 物理有效性本身不足以作为可靠物理推理的代理指标；PVPO方法能有效解决PhysHack问题，实现更可靠的物理感知生成。

Abstract: LLM-based LEGO assembly generation requires both semantic grounding and physical feasibility. We identify a data-induced failure mode, PhysHack, in which the assemblies satisfy physical-validity constraints while producing structures that are geometrically misaligned, semantically inconsistent, or poorly calibrated. To address this challenge, we propose a model-based data selection approach that uses only a small fraction of the training data while improving physically grounded LEGO assembly generation. Building on the selected trajectories, we introduce PVPO, a sample-efficient reinforcement learning method that couples physical feasibility with voxel-space geometric rewards. Our results show that physical validity alone is an insufficient proxy for reliable physical reasoning: models can learn to generate valid structures without preserving semantic or geometric fidelity. Experiments across model backbones and test-time scaling settings demonstrate that PVPO improves structural and semantic alignment, physical validity, structural stability, and calibration, while reducing reliance on extensive post-hoc rejection sampling. In particular, results on calibration show that PVPO mitigates PhysHack by making test-time selection more predictive of semantic and structural quality.

</details>


### [92] [MetaEvo: A Meta-Optimization Framework for Experience-Driven Agent Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07603)
*Bowen Ren,Heyan Huang,Yinghao Li,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MetaEvo提出两阶段持续智能体进化框架，通过偏好优化增强模型原则抽象能力，并结合模块化架构实现原则的积累复用，在多种推理基准上持续超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的智能体静态部署、无法通过任务交互持续改进；经验驱动方法仅依赖记忆或启发式，将模型视为被动执行者，导致性能过早停滞、长期提升有限。

Method: 两阶段框架：1) 基于偏好优化提升模型的原则抽象能力；2) 在模块化智能体架构中实现原则的积累与复用。

Result: 在多个推理基准上持续优于强基线，迭代改善稳定可靠。

Conclusion: 元优化能够有效使智能体从经验中学习并持续增强推理能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit strong reasoning capabilities, yet most LLM-based agents are statically deployed and unable to improve through task interactions. Existing experience-driven methods often rely on memory or heuristics without enhancing the model's ability to learn, treating it as a passive executor and leading to early performance plateaus and limited long-term improvement. To address this issue, we propose MetaEvo, a two-stage framework for continual agent evolution that focuses on improving how the model learns from tasks experience, rather than solely on what it stores. MetaEvo first applies preference-based optimization to enhance the model's ability of principle abstraction, then enables the accumulation and reuse of these principles within a modular agent architecture. Experimental results on diverse reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that MetaEvo consistently outperforms strong baselines, maintains reliable improvement across iterations. These findings validate the effectiveness of meta-optimization in enabling agents to learn from experience and continually enhance their reasoning capabilities.

</details>


### [93] [LEAF: Growing Trees Without Branching for Speech-Aware Large Language Model Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07610)
*Argyrios Gerogiannis,Yekaterina Yegorova,Mark Hasegawa-Johnson,Venugopal V. Veeravalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LEAF方法，通过回溯式树结构在语音感知大模型后训练中实现span级别的信用分配，无需在线分支或额外解码，在语音问答和翻译任务上超越GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GRPO风格的语音大模型后训练方法存在粗粒度信用分配问题，对所有token使用相同的终端奖励，忽视了rollout批次中语音条件补全的树状结构信息（共享前缀后在不同决策点分叉）。

Method: LEAF方法包括：采样完整响应、选择高困惑度边界、按共享前缀分组响应、利用后代奖励分配span级别优势。该方法无需在线分支或额外解码，基于低秩适应（LoRA）预算。

Result: 在相同的rollout和低秩适应预算下，LEAF在语音问答和语音翻译基准上均优于GRPO。更小规模的LEAF训练模型超越了当前全参数微调的最先进基线。

Conclusion: LEAF通过回溯式树结构恢复rollout批次内的结构化信息，实现了更精细的span级信用分配，在语音大模型后训练中达到更优性能，且计算开销与GRPO相当。

Abstract: State-of-the-art GRPO-style methods for speech-aware large language model post-training suffer from coarse credit assignment, broadcasting the same terminal-reward advantage to every token in a response. This ignores useful structure within rollout batches, where speech-conditioned completions often share prefixes before diverging at important decisions. We propose Low-rank Exploration with Adaptive Forking (LEAF), a retrospective tree-based RL method that recovers this structure without online branching or additional decoding. LEAF samples complete responses, selects high-surprisal boundaries, groups responses by shared prefixes, and assigns span-level advantages using descendant rewards. We theoretically justify LEAF's span-level credit assignment and boundary-selection design. Empirically, LEAF improves over GRPO across speech question answering and speech translation benchmarks under the same rollout and low-rank adaptation budget. Notably, smaller LEAF-trained models outperform current state-of-the-art, full-parameter baselines.

</details>


### [94] [Graph Neural Networks for Predicting Solvability of Finite Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07619)
*Tal Weissblat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种图神经网络框架，通过有限群的图表示（如Cayley图）来分类群的可解性，仅利用结构图信息区分可解群与不可解群，并在训练集之外的群上评估其泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索图神经网络能否从图表示中学习有限群的代数性质（如可解性），并考察代数结构与图几何表示之间的关系。

Method: 使用图神经网络框架，以有限群关联的图表示（包括Cayley图）作为输入，训练模型仅依靠结构图信息来区分可解群与不可解群。

Result: 在训练集之外的群上评估了模型性能，展示了GNN能够学习有限群的代数性质。

Conclusion: 该研究作为概念验证，表明GNN能够从基于图的表示中学习有限群的代数性质，为代数结构与图几何表示之间的关系提供了新见解。

Abstract: We present a Graph Neural Network (GNN) framework for the classification of finite groups according to their solvability. Using graph representations associated with finite groups, including Cayley graphs (CG), the proposed model is trained to distinguish solvable and non-solvable groups using structural graph information alone. The framework is evaluated on groups outside the training dataset in order to investigate the extent to which GNNs can learn algebraic properties arising in group theory. More broadly, the present work explores the relationship between algebraic structure and graph-based geometric representations of finite groups. The present study is intended as a proof-of-concept investigation of whether GNNs can learn algebraic properties of finite groups from graph-based representations

</details>


### [95] [Sequential statistical inference for Large Language Models: Representation, validity, and monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07624)
*Yao Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将序贯统计推断应用于LLM可信赖性部署，通过将LLM交互建模为依赖随机过程（而非独立提示-响应对），在表示、验证和监控三个层面实现统计过程控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在部署中面临重复查询、上下文演化、用户/工具反馈、模型更新后行为偏移等问题，现有研究多将LLM交互视为孤立提示-响应对，缺乏对依赖性和动态变化的统计建模。

Method: 围绕三个任务展开：(1) 表示——将LLM交互建模为依赖随机过程；(2) 验证——在依赖、重复使用和自适应条件下开发有意义的不确定性保证；(3) 监控——利用序贯警报和变化点检测识别校准、幻觉率、拒绝行为、公平性等属性的偏移。

Result: 提出将可信赖LLM部署视为统计过程控制问题的视角，补充了现有综述研究。

Conclusion: 序贯统计推断能够自然贡献于LLM可信赖性，为处理LLM部署中的动态依赖性和行为偏移提供了系统性统计框架。

Abstract: This discussion argues that sequential statistical inference can naturally contribute to LLM trustworthiness. In deployment, LLM systems are queried repeatedly, conditioned on evolving contexts, and incorporate user or tool feedback, and may exhibit behavioral shifts after model updates or distribution changes. The discussion is organized around three tasks: representation, modeling LLM interactions as dependent stochastic processes rather than isolated prompt--response pairs; validity, developing uncertainty guarantees that remain meaningful under dependence, repeated use, and adaptation; and monitoring, using sequential alarms and change-point detection to identify shifts in calibration, hallucination rates, refusal behavior, fairness, or other task-relevant properties. This perspective complements recent surveys by viewing trustworthy LLM deployment as a problem of statistical process control.

</details>


### [96] [Active Learning with Foundation Model Priors: Efficient Learning under Class Imbalance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07630)
*Jiancheng Zhang,Meiqing Li,Qi Zhang,Yinglun Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用基础模型先验知识的主动学习框架，能够同时处理类别不平衡和标签噪声问题，在图像和文本领域均实现超过50%的标注成本节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实数据集常存在类别分布倾斜和标注噪声双重问题，导致模型性能下降，尤其在少数类上表现更差。现有主动学习方法多只关注单一挑战，缺乏系统性应对两者共存场景的方案。

Method: 提出创新的主动学习框架，利用基础模型先验知识，实现基础模型与小模型之间的不平衡感知联合决策，从而选择信息量最大且类别平衡的样本进行标注。

Result: 在图像和文本领域的不平衡数据集上，该方法相比最优主动学习基线节省超过50%的标注成本，同时保持模型性能和对标签噪声的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 这是首次系统性地研究在标签噪声和类别不平衡双重挑战下的主动学习，验证了基础模型先验在应对复杂数据质量问题时的高效性。

Abstract: Real-world datasets across image and text domains are often characterized by skewed class distributions and noisy annotations, which jointly degrade model performance, particularly on minority classes. Among existing solutions, active learning offers an effective and efficient paradigm by selectively querying the most informative and balanced samples for annotation. We propose an innovative active learning framework that mitigates class imbalance and selects the most informative samples to annotate. Leveraging foundation model priors, our algorithm enables imbalance-aware co-decisions between foundation model and small model to tackle noisy and imbalanced labels across various domains. We introduce the first study to systematically explore active learning under the dual challenges of label noise and class imbalance across image and text domains. Extensive experiments on imbalanced datasets demonstrate that our method achieves substantial annotation savings-over 50% compared to the best active learning baseline-while preserving performance and robustness to label noise.

</details>


### [97] [Semantic Cache Distillation: Efficient State Transfer via Reuse and Selective Patching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07684)
*Qianli Ma,Zhiqing Tang,Hanshuai Cui,Zhi Yao,Weijia Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出语义缓存蒸馏（SCD）框架，通过紧凑语义编码替代原始KV缓存传输，在LLM分片服务中实现高达2.65倍的首 Token 延迟加速，同时将生成质量控制在F1分数5%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM分片服务中KV缓存传输造成严重通信瓶颈，且异构模型间缓存重用会导致语义错位累积，降低生成质量。

Method: 提出SCD框架，包含两个机制：(1) Reuse：从低秩子空间重建大部分层以最小化传输成本；(2) Patch：在稀疏过渡层预测归一化输入以截断错误传播。

Result: 相比oracle consumer预填充，TTFT加速高达2.65倍；在带宽受限场景下，在质量-延迟帕累托前沿上优于量化和选择性重计算基线；生成质量F1分数与oracle差距在5%以内。

Conclusion: SCD有效缓解了LLM分片服务中的通信瓶颈和语义错位问题，实现了通信效率与生成质量的良好权衡。

Abstract: Disaggregated serving alleviates memory bottlenecks in Large Language Model (LLM) inference but creates a severe communication bottleneck: transmitting high-dimensional Key-Value (KV) caches often dominates time-to-first-token (TTFT). Moreover, reusing caches across heterogeneous models (e.g., base and fine-tuned variants) causes semantic misalignment that accumulates over layers, degrading generation quality. We propose Semantic Cache Distillation (SCD), a loss-constrained framework that replaces raw KV transmission with compact semantic codes. SCD addresses these challenges via two mechanisms: (1) Reuse, which reconstructs most layers from low-rank subspaces to minimize transfer cost, and (2) Patch, which predicts normalized inputs at sparse transition layers to truncate error propagation. Empirically, SCD delivers up to 2.65 $\times$ TTFT speedup over the oracle consumer prefill and dominates quantization and selective recomputation baselines on the quality--latency Pareto frontier in bandwidth-constrained regimes, while keeping generation quality within 5\% F1 of the oracle.

</details>


### [98] [BCG-FM: A Foundation Model for Ambient Cardiac Health Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07692)
*Magnus Ruud Kjaer,Haejun Han,Ashish Neupane,David Q. Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BCG-FM，首个面向环境机械生物信号的基础模型。利用压电传感器从床面记录心冲击图（BCG），在145,985名受试者的275万小时数据上通过对比学习预训练。冻结嵌入在生物年龄估计上达到3.26年MAE（环境无接触模态最佳），并在15种健康状态和三个外部队列中实现临床相关判别。仅需500名标注样本即超越3,372样本的监督基线，表征质量与对比批次大小呈对数线性增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可穿戴生物信号基础模型均依赖需用户主动操作的模态（佩戴设备或访问睡眠实验室），缺乏无需用户努力的环境感知模态基础模型。

Method: 基于压电传感器嵌入床面记录BCG信号，采用受试者级对比学习，在145,985人共275万小时夜间记录上预训练BCG-FM模型。

Result: 冻结嵌入在生物年龄估计上达3.26年MAE（环境无接触模态最低）；15种自报健康状态及三个外部队列中取得临床级判别；500标注样本超越3,372样本的完全监督基线；表征质量与对比批次大小呈对数线性缩放。

Conclusion: 环境纵向机械生物信号是健康基础模型的可行模态，BCG-FM证明了无用户努力、无需接触即可实现临床级健康表征学习的能力。

Abstract: Foundation models for wearable biosignals have matched or exceeded supervised specialists across a range of clinical tasks, yet all rely on modalities that require deliberate user action--wearing a device or visiting a sleep lab. We introduce BCG-FM, the first foundation model for ambient mechanical biosignals. A piezoelectric sensor embedded in the bed surface records ballistocardiography (BCG) each night without user effort; we pretrain BCG-FM with participant-level contrastive learning and using a total of 2.75 million hours of nightly recordings from 145,985 individuals, the largest raw-waveform biosignal pretraining corpus to date. Frozen BCG-FM embeddings achieve 3.26-year MAE on biological-age estimation (the lowest reported for any ambient, contactless modality) and yield clinically relevant discrimination across 15 self-reported health conditions and three independent external cohorts. Pretrained representations from only 500 labeled participants outperform a fully supervised baseline trained on 3,372, and representation quality scales log-linearly with contrastive batch size. These results establish ambient, longitudinal mechanical biosignals as a viable modality for health foundation models.

</details>


### [99] [Adversarial Robustness of Activation Steering in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07696)
*Kien Le,Thai Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估了激活控制在对抗性文本扰动下的鲁棒性，发现所有方法、模型和人格设定下方向鲁棒性下降高达64%，置信度崩溃至0.25以下，最优层在扰动下偏移多达17层，且现有缓解措施效果有限，表明激活控制的脆弱性是结构性的而非方法特异的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活控制已成为一种流行的免训练方法来控制LLM行为，但其对现实输入变化的鲁棒性尚未被研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统评估激活控制在对抗性文本扰动下的鲁棒性。

Method: 覆盖四种提取方法（PCA、MD等）、三种攻击策略、六种人格（来自Anthropic Model-Written Evaluation Dataset）以及五种参数规模从1.5B到30B的模型，进行系统性评估。

Result: 攻击在所有设定下广泛成功：方向鲁棒性下降高达64%，置信度崩溃至0.25以下，最优层在扰动下偏移多达17层。从对抗扰动输入中提取向量仅能在中大型模型上部分恢复PCA和MD的可控性，但无法找到改进的最优层。

Conclusion: 激活控制的脆弱性是结构性的而非方法特异的，当前层选择策略在实际部署中不够鲁棒。

Abstract: Activation steering has become a popular training-free method to control LLM behavior by injecting precomputed direction vectors into the model's residual stream at inference time. Yet its robustness to realistic input variation remains unstudied. We present the first systematic evaluation of activation steering robustness under adversarial text perturbations on the inputs, covering four extraction methods, three attack strategies, six personas from Anthropic Model-Written Evaluation Dataset, and five models ranging from 1.5B to 30B parameters. Attacks succeed broadly across all settings: directional robustness drops by up to 64%, post-attack confidence collapses near or below 0.25 across all methods and models, and steering strength degrades on nearly every steerable input. Layer selection is equally fragile, with the optimal layer identified by an automated method on clean inputs shifting by up to 17 positions under perturbation, a failure that compounds the vector-level breakdown. Extracting vectors from adversarially perturbed inputs partially recovers steerability for PCA and MD on mid-to-large models, but they consistently fail to locate the improved optimal layer, limiting the practical benefit of this mitigation. Together, these findings reveal that the brittleness of activation steering is structural rather than method-specific, and that current layer selection strategies are not robust enough for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [100] [Pharmacogenomic Knowledge Graph Augmentation for Graph Neural Network-Based Drug-Drug Interaction Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07698)
*Juergen Dietrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探究了药物基因组学先验知识（来自PharmGKB数据库的CYP酶注释）能否突破图神经网络在药物相互作用预测中的信息天花板。结果表明，知识图谱增强在配对级别数据分割下显著提升了DDI类型分类性能（F1-macro从0.241提升至0.532），但二元交互检测和药物级泛化仍受限于信息天花板。机制验证确认增强优先改善CYP2C9介导的交互预测，扩展实验表明效果依赖于药物基因组学注释覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期研究表明GNN在DDI预测中的性能受限于训练标签的结构信息含量——信息天花板（Information Ceiling），仅靠架构改进无法突破。本研究旨在探究药物基因组学先验知识（如CYP酶代谢通路注释）这一独立于分子结构的互补信息能否部分打破这一天花板。

Method: 从PharmGKB数据库提取CYP2D6、CYP3A4、CYP2C19、CYP2C9四种同工酶的底物、抑制剂、诱导剂注释，构建12维特征向量，与分子嵌入拼接后用于交互预测。实验采用配对级别和药物级别两种数据分割方式，并在Tox21基准上进行单分子毒性预测扩展验证。

Result: 配对级别分割下，KG增强将DDI类型分类F1-macro从0.241提升至0.532；二元交互检测和药物级泛化仍受限于信息天花板（AUC从0.250降至0.224）。严格留出化合物验证表明增强优先改善CYP2C9介导的交互预测（概率从0.033-0.117提升至0.560-0.586）。

Conclusion: 药物基因组学知识增强能部分提升DDI类型分类性能，但无法完全突破信息天花板，效果依赖于注释覆盖率。该发现为后续研究提出的多模态框架提供了动机。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) applied to drug-drug interaction (DDI) prediction rely exclusively on molecular structure encoded as SMILES-derived graphs. Prior work in this series demonstrated that model performance is bounded by the structural information content of training labels -- an Information Ceiling -- that architectural refinements alone cannot overcome. The present study investigates whether pharmacogenomic prior knowledge from the PharmGKB database partially closes this ceiling by providing metabolic pathway context that is independent of, and complementary to, molecular structure. Cytochrome P450 (CYP) enzyme substrate, inhibitor, and inducer annotations for four clinically relevant isoforms (CYP2D6, CYP3A4, CYP2C19, CYP2C9) are extracted and incorporated as a 12-dimensional feature vector concatenated to the molecular embedding prior to interaction prediction. Experiments are conducted under both pair-level and drug-level data splits to quantify generalization to unseen drugs. Results indicate that knowledge graph (KG) augmentation substantially improves DDI type classification under pair-level split conditions (F1-macro: 0.532 vs. 0.241 baseline), while binary interaction detection and drug-level generalization remain bounded by the Information Ceiling (AUC inflation: 0.224 vs. 0.250 baseline). Mechanistic validation on strictly held-out compounds confirms that augmentation preferentially improves CYP2C9-mediated interaction prediction, with probabilities increasing from 0.033-0.117 (baseline) to 0.560-0.586 (KG-augmented). An extension to single-molecule toxicity prediction on the Tox21 benchmark confirms that the effect is contingent on pharmacogenomic annotation coverage. These findings motivate the multimodal framework proposed for the subsequent study in this series.

</details>


### [101] [EssentialGIN: a new approach for gene essentiality prediction based on graph isomorphism neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07700)
*Sahar Mansouri-Rad,Zahra Narimani,Parvin Razzaghi,Nazanin Hosseinkhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图同构网络（GIN）的深度架构，通过整合PPI网络拓扑特征与基因表达、基因同源性和亚细胞定位等生物学数据，显著提升了必需基因预测的准确性，尤其在人类数据上优于传统中心性方法、Node2Vec、MLP和图注意力网络等方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于中心性度量的计算方法预测必需基因准确率低、假阳性率高，而湿实验成本高昂且耗时，因此需要更精确的计算模型来缩小候选基因范围。

Method: 采用改进的图同构网络架构，以PPI网络中的蛋白质为节点，嵌入节点特征时既保留网络拓扑结构，又整合基因表达、基因同源性和亚细胞定位等生物学信息。

Result: 实验表明该方法优于基线中心性方法、Node2Vec、MLP以及图注意力网络（GAT）。在E. coli和D. melanogaster等简单生物中，基于Node2Vec嵌入的MLP表现也较好，但在人类（H. sapiens）数据上，该架构显著优于其他深度学习和图神经网络方法。

Conclusion: 集成生物学数据的图同构网络能够同时利用网络拓扑和节点属性信息，显著提升必需基因预测的准确率，特别是在复杂生物中优势更加明显。

Abstract: Background: Prediction of essential genes (proteins), is a basic and challenging problem but at the same time very costly and time-consuming in wet-lab experiments. Predicting essential genes, only based on computational methods (to introduce wet-lab candidates) using centrality measures are not accurate and result in large number of false positives; therefore, more complex models such as deep learning and also integration of biological information are used in recent research to identify essential genes.
  Methods: In this work we focus on graph isomorphism networks, in order to embed proteins as a node in PPI network to conserve topological features of PPI network, and also integrate biological data such as gene expression data, gene orthology information and gene subcellular localization information, and introduced a deep architecture for predicting essential genes. Graph isomorphism network architecture is modified in this work for embedding node information.
  Results: Our experiments proved that the proposed method outperforms baseline centrality-based methods and also machine learning based methods such as Node2Vec, MLP, and also graph attention networks (GAT).
  Conclusion: In this paper we observed that using graph isomorphism networks that integrate biological data (as node attributes) and preserve network topology can significantly improve the essential gene prediction accuracy. In simpler organisms such as E. coli and D. melanogaster, methods such as multi-layer perceptron using Node2Vec embedding also performs very good, but in H. sapiens the introduced architecture significantly outperforms deep learning and other graph neural network solutions.
  Keywords: Essential gene prediction, graph neural network, graph isomorphism network, PPI network, node embedding

</details>


### [102] [FunctionEvolve: Structure-Guided Symbolic Regression with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07704)
*Zeyu Xia,Jun Zhu,Dong Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FunctionEvolve提出了一种基于表达式树的结构感知进化框架用于符号回归，利用LLM引导变异但保持结构可见性，在129个合成任务上恢复107个精确公式，SA@50达82.9%，是同类基线的4.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的符号回归系统存在结构盲区：候选解不透明、缺乏局部变异机制、系数拟合脆弱导致正确骨架被低估。需要一种既能利用LLM语义引导又能保持显式结构操作的框架。

Method: 采用表达式树组织搜索，包含：1) 结构摘要促进多样化父本选择；2) 局部树编辑保留有用子表达式；3) 结构感知的系数拟合（分解、约束、简化）实现更可靠的评分。仅使用初等函数族，无额外领域规则限制泛化性。

Result: 在LLM-SRBench的129个合成任务上，使用Claude Opus 4.6恢复107个精确公式（82.9% SA@50），是相同骨干基线的4.5倍；SA@1达55.8%，是最强已发表top-1结果的3.6倍。

Conclusion: 结构可见的搜索是实现可靠符号恢复的核心；LLM引导的细化和结构感知的系数优化分别作为必要的提议和评分机制。此外，审计发现基准中材料科学子集的共线性导致可辨识性问题。

Abstract: Symbolic regression aims to uncover explicit scientific laws from data. Recent methods use LLMs to guide mutation from background text, which is more directed than random genetic programming. However, exact symbolic recovery requires both semantic guidance and explicit structure, so that domain-informed search are carried out through valid symbolic representation. Current LLM-driven systems remain structure-blind: they select among opaque candidates, lack explicit mechanisms for local mutation, and rely on brittle coefficient fitting that can undervalue correct skeletons. We propose FunctionEvolve, an evolutionary framework using expression trees to organize the whole search: structural summaries promote diverse parent selection, local tree edits preserve useful subexpressions, and structure-aware fitting decomposes, constrains, and simplifies coefficients for more reliable scoring. It uses only elementary function families, without additional domain-specific rules limiting generalization. On the 129-task synthetic subset of LLM-SRBench, FunctionEvolve with \emph{Claude Opus 4.6} recovers 107 exact forms, reaching 82.9% SA@50, 4.5x above same-backbone baselines, and 55.8% SA@1, 3.6x above the strongest previously published top-1 result. Ablations show that structure-visible search is central to reliable recovery, with LLM-guided refinements and structure-aware coefficient optimization serving as essential proposal and scoring mechanisms. We also audit the benchmark and show that collinearity in its materials-science subset creates identifiability issues.

</details>


### [103] [SAW: Stage-Aware Dynamic Weighting for Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07705)
*Yuchen He,Baolong Bi,Shenghua Liu,Huaming Liao,Yuyao Ge,Bolin Wan,Siqian Tong,Juan Chen,Jiafeng Guo,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Stage-Aware Dynamic Weighting (SAW)，一种轻量级动态加权机制，通过变异系数（CV）衡量各奖励维度的实时信息量，解决多目标强化学习中不同目标学习进度异步导致的噪声干扰问题，在GRPO和GDPO框架下均能提升训练效率和最终性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标强化学习（MORL）中对齐大语言模型与复杂人类偏好时，静态加权求和忽略了奖励学习在不同目标间存在显著异步性：学习好的维度产生低方差同质信号，其残余噪声会污染聚合奖励或占据固定优势预算份额，干扰学习不足维度携带的高价值信号。

Method: 提出SAW机制，利用变异系数（CV）作为尺度不变的实时信息量代理，在batch内根据各维度的相对信息量重新加权其奖励或优势贡献。该方法仅依赖batch级统计量，无需多次前向/反向传播，计算开销极小。

Result: 在工具调用和文本摘要任务上，SAW在GRPO和GDPO两种框架下均一致地提升了训练效率和最终性能。

Conclusion: SAW是一种通用即插即用的多奖励LLM对齐插件，能够有效缓解多目标学习异步问题，且计算开销可忽略不计。

Abstract: Although multi-objective reinforcement learning (MORL) is central to aligning large language models with complex human preferences, the prevailing practice of static weighted summation overlooks a more fundamental phenomenon: reward learning is markedly asynchronous across objectives. Well-learned dimensions quickly produce homogeneous, low-variance signals whose residual noise contaminates the aggregated reward (in GRPO) or occupies a fixed share of the advantage budget (in GDPO), interfering with the scarce yet high-value signals carried by under-learned dimensions. To address this asynchrony, we propose Stage-Aware Dynamic Weighting (SAW), a lightweight, algorithm-agnostic dynamic weighting mechanism. SAW utilizes the coefficient of variation (CV) as a scale-invariant proxy for real-time informativeness, reweighting each dimension's reward or advantage contribution by its relative informativeness within the batch. Unlike gradient-based methods that require multiple forward and backward passes, SAW relies solely on batch-level statistics, introducing nearly negligible computational overhead. Experiments on tool-calling and text summarization tasks demonstrate that SAW consistently improves both training efficiency and final performance under both GRPO and GDPO frameworks, confirming it as a general-purpose plug-in for multi-reward LLM alignment. Our code is available at https://github.com/Zhaolutuan/SAW

</details>


### [104] [Decoding Naturalistic Emotion Dynamics from the Brain: An LLM-Enhanced Regression Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07707)
*Lemei Zhang,Peng Liu,Hans Dahle Kvadsheim,August Sætre Aasvær,Shuer Ye,Reza Bonyadi,Maryam Ziaei,Jon Atle Gulla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将情绪解码从离散分类重构为连续多维度回归框架，利用LLM自动标注自然主义叙事语料的情感轨迹，结合动态功能连接与可解释AI技术，证明分布式网络动态交互比传统定位主义方法更具情绪解释力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统情绪解码采用离散单标签分类，过于简化了人类情感的连续性、流动性和共现性。本文旨在通过多目标回归框架追踪随时间连续变化的多重情绪维度，更真实地建模情感动态。

Method: 1）利用LLM从自然听觉叙事《爱丽丝梦游仙境》中提取细粒度连续情感轮廓作为主观情感的替代标注；2）采用正则化和基于核的机器学习算法作为连续估计器，追踪宏观神经状态变化幅度；3）比较动态功能连接(DFC)快照与静态ROI幅度表征；4）引入图论可解释AI(XAI)技术解析情绪特异性的拓扑配置。

Result: 基于DFC时间快照训练的模型显著优于静态ROI幅度表征，能有效捕捉快速波动叙事输入下的连续情绪轨迹；通过图论XAI揭示了高度可解释的情绪特异性拓扑配置。

Conclusion: 结果支持LLM自动化标注在情感神经科学中的实用性，为心理建构主义框架提供了实证证据，表明动态分布式网络交互比严格定位主义对情绪的解释力更强。

Abstract: Decoding emotional states from neural signals has been typically framed as a discrete, single-label classification task based on emotionally stable stimuli, a formulation that oversimplifies the continuous, fluid, and co-occurring nature of human affect. This study reconceptualizes emotion decoding by adopting a multi-target regression framework to track multiple overlapping emotional dimensions as continuous trajectories over time. Leveraging the robust generalization capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs), we extracted fine-grained, continuous sentiment profiles from a naturalistic auditory narrative, Alice in Wonderland, to serve as scalable proxies for subjective affect from human fMRI dataset. Departing from standard classification paradigms or mass-univariate subtractive contrasts that filter out network dynamics, we leverage regularized and kernel-based machine learning algorithms as continuous estimators to track the magnitude of macroscale neural state variations. We demonstrate that models trained on temporal snapshots of Dynamic Functional Connectivity (DFC) significantly outperform static region-of-interest (ROI) amplitude representations, effectively capturing continuous emotional trajectories under rapidly fluctuating narrative input. Furthermore, by implementing graph-theoretical Explainable AI (XAI) techniques, we deconstruct the underlying predictive features to reveal highly interpretable, emotion-specific topological configurations. Collectively, these results highlight the utility of LLM-automated annotation in affective neuroscience and provide compelling empirical evidence for psychological constructionist frameworks, demonstrating that dynamic, distributed network interactions offer superior explanatory power over strictly locationist accounts of emotion.

</details>


### [105] [Rosetta Memory: Adaptive Memory for Cross-LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07711)
*Hao Yang,Shiqi Shen,Haoxuan Li,Zhipeng Wang,Zhi Gong,Xu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出从"以LLM为中心的记忆设计"转向"以记忆为中心的LLM适配"，通过双端（写/读）Profile条件化操作符和最小增益采样课程，解决跨模型记忆迁移问题，在多个多跳QA数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆系统以LLM为中心设计，但用户经常在不同LLM之间切换（如Claude编码、GPT写作），导致一个模型写入的记忆难以被另一个模型有效读取和利用，而跨模型记忆适配问题尚未被充分研究。

Method: 提出记忆为中心的LLM适配框架，包含：(1) 写端和读端的Profile条件化操作符，联合训练优化记忆存储与呈现；(2) 最小增益采样课程，优先训练对最不获益LLM的操作符；(3) 性能差距奖励，对比朴素记忆基线来衡量操作符的实际贡献。

Result: 在HotpotQA、2WikiMultihopQA和MuSiQue数据集上，所提模型一致优于基线方法，且在未见过的模型替换下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 从记忆中心视角设计跨LLM适配机制是解决模型切换场景下记忆复用问题的有效路径，所提方法具有良好的泛化能力和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Memory is the key component for transforming a stateless LLM into a persistent, evolving agent through experience accumulation, long-horizon planning, and continual self-improvement. Existing memory systems typically take the LLM as the center and design memory operations tailored to a specific backbone. In practice, however, users frequently switch between LLMs, for example using Claude for coding and GPT for writing across tasks, or routing different steps to different backbones within a single task for cost-effective trade-offs. As a result, memory written by one model often needs to be consumed by another. Making upstream memory effectively adapt to and activate downstream LLMs remains a critical yet underexplored problem. To bridge this gap, we shift the perspective from LLM-centric memory design to \emph{memory-centric LLM adaptation}. Specifically, we approach the above upstream-downstream memory adaptation problem from both the write and read sides, and design two profile-conditioned operators that are jointly trained to optimize how memory is stored and presented for better task completion. To ensure the learned operators generalize across a broad set of LLMs, we propose a minimum-gain sampling curriculum that prioritizes the least-served LLMs during training. To better measure the operators' actual contribution rather than the LLM's own capability, we design a performance-gap reward that compares against a naive memory baseline. Experiments on HotpotQA, 2WikiMultihopQA, and MuSiQue demonstrate that our model consistently outperforms baselines and remains robust under unseen-model replacement.

</details>


### [106] [Cutting LLM Evaluation Costs with SySRs: A Bandit Algorithm that Provably Exploits Model Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07726)
*Zifan Lyu,Chahine Nejma,Tobias Wegel,Fanny Yang,Florian E. Dorner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Synchronized Successive Rejects (SySR)算法，通过在经典Successive Rejects算法中引入配对比较，利用模型间的相似性来高效识别最优语言模型，无需超参数调优，且在理论上和实验上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基准测试对所有模型评估所有查询效率低下，尤其当某些模型明显较差时无需精确评估其性能。最佳臂识别算法可自适应分配预算降低成本，但现有方法未能有效利用语言模型对同一提示的响应相似性这一特性。

Method: 提出Synchronized Successive Rejects (SySR)算法，在经典Successive Rejects框架中引入配对比较（paired comparisons）机制，利用模型间的相似性进行同步淘汰，无需超参数调优。

Result: 在15个标准基准上，SySR在平均错误率和可靠识别最优模型的最坏情况预算方面均优于所有基线方法。

Conclusion: SySR通过配对比较有效利用了模型相似性，实现了无超参数的高效最佳模型识别，且性能保证随模型相似度提高而增强。

Abstract: Large Language Models are typically benchmarked by evaluating every model on every test query. For practitioners seeking the best model to deploy, this is often wasteful: if a model clearly performs worse than others, there is no need to precisely estimate its performance. Best-arm identification algorithms can be naturally applied to drastically reduce costs by adaptively allocating evaluation budget. Further, language models often respond similarly to the same prompt-a property previous work has tried to leverage with mixed success. We propose Synchronized Successive Rejects (SySRs), augmenting the classical Successive Rejects algorithm with paired comparisons. Unlike prior attempts to leverage model similarity in best-model identification, our approach is hyperparameter-free and enjoys performance guarantees that improve with the degree of similarity between evaluated models. Empirically, our method outperforms all baselines in terms of average error rate across 15 standard benchmarks, and in terms of worst-case budget for reliably identifying the best model.

</details>


### [107] [Byzantine Cheap Talk: Adversarial Resilience and Topology Effects in LLM Coordination Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07790)
*Aya El Mir,Martin Takáč,Salem Lahlou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多智能体LLM系统中通信协议的鲁棒性，发现在4人Stag Hunt游戏中存在两类脆弱性：(1) Byzantine攻击下智能体虽能检测背叛但无法集体适应；(2) 明确限制通信拓扑会破坏协作，而静默应用相同限制则保持近乎完美的协作。同时识别出两种稳定的行为原型：易背叛型和协作坚持型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管廉价谈话（cheap-talk）通道已被证明能促进LLM协作博弈中的合作，但多智能体LLM系统在对抗性和结构性约束下的鲁棒性尚未得到充分理解。

Method: 在4人Stag Hunt博弈中，跨6个模型系列和720次试验，研究两类脆弱性：(1) Byzantine攻击场景——恶意智能体发出合作信号但实际背叛；(2) 通信拓扑限制——明确vs静默地限制智能体间的通信结构。

Result: (1) Byzantine攻击下非恶意智能体能在一轮内检测到背叛，但因博弈的一致同意（unanimity）收益结构无法集体恢复协调，部分智能体持续被利用；(2) 明确限制通信拓扑破坏协作，但静默应用相同限制却能保持近乎完美的协作，表明协调失败源于智能体对隐藏信息的元推理而非信息损失本身；(3) 识别出两类稳定的行为原型：易背叛型（背叛后永久切换）和协作坚持型（即使付出巨大个体代价仍继续协作）。

Conclusion: 揭示了具体的系统安全漏洞：通信通道可被利用为对抗性注入向量，向智能体披露网络拓扑结构即使在没有攻击者的情况下也会降低协作性能。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems increasingly rely on communication protocols for coordination, yet their robustness under adversarial and structural constraints remains poorly understood. Building on prior work showing that cheap-talk channels enable cooperation in LLM coordination games, we investigate two vulnerability classes in a 4-player Stag Hunt across six model families and 720 trials. First, when Byzantine agents signal cooperation but defect, non-Byzantine agents detect the betrayal within one round yet fail to adapt collectively: a substantial fraction continue cooperating despite repeated exploitation, unable to recover coordination due to the game's unanimity payoff structure. Second, explicitly restricting communication topology collapses cooperation, while applying identical restrictions silently preserves near-perfect cooperation. This establishes that coordination failure stems from agents' meta-reasoning about hidden information, not information loss itself. We identify two stable behavioral archetypes that replicate across all model cohorts: Defection-Prone models that switch permanently after betrayal, and Cooperation-Persistent models that continue cooperating at significant individual cost. These findings reveal concrete security vulnerabilities: communication channels can be exploited as adversarial injection vectors, and disclosing network topology to agents can degrade coordination even without any adversary present.

</details>


### [108] [Strained Coherence: A Pre-Failure Signal in Coding Agent Execution Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07889)
*Marut Pandya,Kasey Zhang,Baiqing Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 定义了LLM编码Agent中一种安全失效模式——“不连贯连贯（strained coherence）”，即智能体识别到问题却仍按错误方式行动。构建了Claude Sonnet 4.6评判器，在Terminal-bench-2轨迹上达到94%失败率预测精度（vs未标记轨迹46%），提供可解释的span级输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码Agent有时会承认自身推理中存在矛盾或问题，但仍然按照有问题的路径执行，这种模式构成安全相关失效。现有检测方法多为词法基线或单变量预测器，缺乏可解释的细粒度检测能力。作者旨在形式化定义该模式并构建可解释的检测器。

Method: 形式化定义“strained coherence”模式；使用Claude Sonnet 4.6作为评判器，读取完整轨迹并标记该模式出现的片段；在Terminal-bench-2数据集上使用Qwen3.5-35B-A3B骨干模型评估；与词法话语标记基线进行比较；在Gemma4-31B上复现验证；进行消融实验（如软化解冲突标记的改写测试）。

Result: 被标记轨迹失败率94% vs 未标记46%（47点差距，p=0.003）；剔除3个提示内嵌示例后46点差距（p=0.006）；在匹配选择性下，检测器精度94% vs 词法基线88%；两种方法交集的10条轨迹失败率达100%（95% CI [69%, 100%]）；在Gemma上复现方向一致但不显著（20点差距，p=0.31），高冗长度子集差距+30点；首次标记出现在轨迹中位数83-84%处；二进制标记在8/8条冲突标记软化的改写轨迹中仍保留。

Conclusion: Strained coherence是LLM编码Agent中一种可检测、可操作的安全失效模式。所提出的检测器相比词法基线具有更高精度，且产出可解释的span级输出（引用的承认、引用的行动、冲突类型），揭示了智能体“看见却忽视”的内容。该方法在低思考内容（zero think content）场景下受限，提示了Verbose思考对检测的重要性。

Abstract: LLM-based coding agents sometimes acknowledge a problem in their own reasoning and then proceed anyway. We call this pattern strained coherence: a safety-relevant failure mode in which an agent has information that should change its behavior, states that information, and still acts against it. The pattern overlaps with verbalized reward hacking, where an agent names a tension between a task proxy and the underlying goal yet optimizes the proxy anyway. We give an operational definition, build a Claude Sonnet 4.6 judge that reads full trajectories and flags spans where the pattern occurs, and evaluate it on 44 Terminal-bench-2 trajectories using a Qwen3.5-35B-A3B backbone. Flagged trajectories fail 94% of the time versus 46% for unflagged trajectories (47-point gap, Fisher's exact p = 0.003; 46 points after excluding three prompt-embedded examples, p = 0.006). At matched selectivity, the detector reaches 94% precision versus 88% for a lexical discourse-marker baseline; the 10-trajectory intersection of the two methods has a 100% failure rate (Clopper-Pearson 95% CI [69%, 100%]). We replicate on Gemma4-31B with 43 trajectories: the overall signal is directionally consistent but not significant (20-point gap, p = 0.31), with attenuation driven largely by 13 trajectories with zero think content, where the detector has no substrate to analyze. In the high-verbosity Gemma tertile, the gap is +30 points; in the mid- and high-verbosity Qwen tertiles, it is +40 points each. The first flag appears at a median of 83-84% of elapsed trajectory time across both models, and the binary flag survives paraphrases that soften explicit conflict markers (8/8 trajectories). Unlike univariate predictors, the detector emits interpretable span-level output -- quoted acknowledgment, quoted action, and typed conflict -- showing what the agent saw and ignored.

</details>


### [109] [CAAL: Contextual Bandits based Online Hand-Craft Active Learning Strategy Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07910)
*Shao-An Yin,Jiacong Li,Tianpei Xie,Cecile Levasseur,Wojciech Kowalinski,Nicola Elia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为上下文自适应主动学习（CAAL）的框架，将每种手工策略视为一个“臂”，利用外部上下文信息进行奖励预测，动态选择标注批次数据的策略，在公共数据集上优于现有基线方法，且结果不受批次大小影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动学习算法面临未标注数据统计分布不确定性的挑战，难以选择最佳的手工策略。

Method: CAAL中每个“臂”代表一种手工策略，通过外部上下文信息进行奖励预测，动态选择标注数据批次的策略，与仅基于标注数据反馈选择策略的现有框架不同。

Result: CAAL在公共数据集上使用所设计的奖励和上下文设计方案，优于现有基线自适应策略，且结果在不同批次大小下保持一致。

Conclusion: CAAL是一个通用框架，可结合领域知识设计更有效的奖励和上下文候选，并实验证明了其有效性。

Abstract: The challenge with active learning algorithms is the uncertainty of the statistical distribution of unlabeled data, making it difficult to choose the best hand-crafted strategy. To address this, we introduced Contextual Adaptive Active Learning (CAAL). In CAAL, each "arm" represents a hand-crafted strategy. Unlike existing frameworks that select strategies based only on feedback from labeled data, we dynamically choose strategies for labeling batches of data using reward prediction with external context information. This general framework allows for customization with domain knowledge to design more effective rewards and context candidates. In addition, we experimentally show that CAAL outperforms the existing baseline adaptive strategy on public datasets using our reward and context design. Our results are consistent regardless of batch size in each iteration.

</details>


### [110] [The Easy, the Hard, and the Learnable: Confidence and Difficulty-Adaptive Policy Optimization for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07950)
*Zhanke Zhou,Xiangyu Lu,Chentao Cao,Brando Miranda,Tongliang Liu,Bo Han,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoDaPO方法，通过置信度和难度自适应地重加权策略更新并重采样高价值可学习问题，以解决GRPO训练中均匀采样和加权导致的计算效率低下问题，在12个基准上持续优于现有RL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准GRPO训练对易、难、可学习问题采用均匀采样和加权，导致计算分配效率低下。通过跟踪token对数概率、组归一化优势和token级更新权重，发现训练中存在置信度膨胀、优势收缩和层次收敛三种动态，表明更新效用强烈依赖于问题难度和模型当前能力。

Method: 提出Confidence and Difficulty-adaptive Policy Optimization (CoDaPO)，从生成置信度和经验难度为每个问题分配有界值，用于重加权策略更新，并在小批次内重采样高价值的可学习问题，从而在固定计算预算下增加可学习带内的探索。

Result: 在12个基准测试上，CoDaPO持续优于现有强化学习方法，并公开代码。

Conclusion: 通过自适应地关注高价值可学习问题而非均匀处理所有问题，CoDaPO显著提升了GRPO框架下的推理能力训练效率。

Abstract: RL with verifiable rewards can substantially improve LLM reasoning, yet standard GRPO-style training often treats easy, hard, and learnable questions alike through uniform sampling and weighting, leading to inefficient compute allocation. We study GRPO by tracking token log-probabilities, group-normalized advantages, and the induced token-level update weights. This reveals three recurring dynamics as training proceeds: (1) confidence inflation, (2) advantage contraction, and (3) hierarchical convergence. These findings suggest that the utility of each update depends strongly on both question difficulty and the model's current competence. Motivated by this, we propose Confidence and Difficulty-adaptive Policy Optimization (CoDaPO), which assigns each question a bounded value from rollout confidence and empirical difficulty. CoDaPO then uses this value to reweight policy updates and resample high-value learnable questions within mini-batches, thereby increasing discovery within the learnable band under a fixed compute budget. Across twelve benchmarks, CoDaPO consistently improves accuracy over existing RL methods. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/tmlr-group/CoDaPO.

</details>


### [111] [Semantic Quorum Assurance: Collective Certification for Non-Deterministic AI Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08021)
*Jun He,Deying Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出语义法定共识(SQA)，一种用于治理非确定性LLM智能体基础设施的控制平面原语。SQA通过声明式执行合同、加密证据链、多样化验证智能体面板和风险自适应法定谓词，将不安全审批率从18.5%降至0.3%，验证延迟为1.45-4.12秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体集成到云操作中时，分布式系统面临语义可靠性问题：智能体可能生成语法有效、静态授权但操作不安全的突变（如修改IAM策略、开放防火墙等）。传统分布式共识协议仅复制确定性状态转换，不评估提议意图的安全性。

Method: SQA将提议表示为绑定加密证据链的声明式执行合同，路由到只读沙盒验证智能体的多样化面板，在风险自适应法定谓词下聚合判断，该谓词强制模型和原型多样性、根据校准的置信度分数调整权重、尊重原型特定否决权。通过的提议通过主权执行门执行。

Result: 在500个基础设施突变场景中，SQA将不安全审批率从单智能体验证的18.5%降至0.3%，风险桶中位数验证延迟为1.45-4.12秒。

Conclusion: SQA有效解决了LLM智能体在云操作中的语义可靠性问题，通过多样性验证和风险自适应机制显著降低了不安全操作的发生率，同时保持可接受的延迟开销。

Abstract: As large language model (LLM) agents are integrated into autonomous cloud operations, distributed systems face a semantic reliability problem: proposer agents can generate production mutations, such as modifying IAM policies, opening firewall security groups, or executing data exports, that are syntactically valid and statically authorized but operationally unsafe. Classical distributed consensus protocols replicate deterministic state transitions but do not evaluate the safety of the proposed intent. To address this gap, we introduce Semantic Quorum Assurance (SQA), a control-plane primitive for governing non-deterministic agentic infrastructure. SQA represents proposals as declarative execution contracts bound to cryptographic evidence chains and routes them to a diverse panel of read-only, sandboxed validator agents. SQA aggregates their judgments under a risk-adaptive quorum predicate that enforces model and archetype diversity, adjusts weights based on calibrated assurance scores, and respects archetype-specific vetoes. Admitted proposals execute only through a sovereign execution gate. We instantiate SQA in a cloud-native control plane and formalize a correlated cognitive failure model for non-deterministic validators. On 500 infrastructure-inspired mutation scenarios, with safety results reported on held-out safe/unsafe trials excluding ambiguous scenarios, SQA reduces unsafe approval from 18.5% for single-agent validation to 0.3% while adding median validation latency of 1.45--4.12 seconds across the studied risk buckets.

</details>


### [112] [When Behavioral Safety Evaluation Fails: A Representation-Level Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08044)
*Enyi Jiang,Anders Gjølbye,Yibo Jacky Zhang,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了"审计差距"概念，即LLM在行为层面的安全性与潜在空间鲁棒性之间的差异。通过构建"解离模型"（保持安全行为但潜在空间脆弱），引入基于干预的评估框架和潜在脆弱性评分（LVS），发现行为安全指标不足以衡量表示层面的鲁棒性，解离模型的LVS显著升高而拒绝行为表现正常，表明中间表示层对干预最为敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM安全性评估主要关注行为层面（输出结果），但忽略了表示层面的鲁棒性（潜在空间中的脆弱性）。作者希望揭示这种评估方式的不完整性，即存在"审计差距"。

Method: 1) 构建"解离模型"——保持安全外向行为的同时在潜在空间中保持脆弱性；2) 提出基于干预的评估框架，包括参数空间和潜在空间的软干预（有害微调、逐层潜在扰动）；3) 提出潜在脆弱性评分（LVS）量化在有限潜在扰动下诱发有害行为的难易程度。

Result: 解离模型在有害干预下LVS显著升高，但拒绝行为与安全模型相当；中间表示层对干预最为敏感；行为安全指标不能充分反映表示层面的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 仅依赖行为安全性评估无法全面反映LLM的鲁棒性，需要结合表示层面感知的审计方法，同时评估潜在脆弱性和可观察行为。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) safety has often been evaluated at the behavior level, which provides limited evidence of internal robustness, as these evaluations target outputs rather than representation-level vulnerability under intervention. We formalize this discrepancy as the audit gap: the difference between behavioral safety and robustness under intervention. To study this gap, we construct dissociated models that preserve safe outward behavior while remaining vulnerable in the latent space. We introduce an intervention-based evaluation framework to test model robustness through soft interventions in parameter and latent spaces, including harmful fine-tuning and layer-wise latent perturbations. To formalize the evaluation, we propose the Latent Vulnerability Score (LVS) to measure how easily harmful behavior can be elicited by bounded latent perturbations. Using this evaluation framework, we show that behavioral safety metrics are insufficient measures of representation-level robustness across multiple safely and unsafely aligned state-of-the-art models. Notably, dissociated models show substantially elevated LVSs despite comparable refusal behavior under harmful intervention, with intermediate representations being the most sensitive to intervention. Our results suggest that behavioral safety evaluation alone provides an incomplete picture of model robustness, motivating representation-aware audits of latent vulnerability and observable behavior.

</details>


### [113] [DICE: Entropy-Regularized Equilibrium Selection for Stable Multi-Agent LLM Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08068)
*Yi Xie,Zhanke Zhou,Chentao Cao,Bo Liu,Bo Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 多智能体大语言模型系统常因均衡选择不当而性能不稳定，本文提出异质性量化响应均衡（HQRE）概念，并基于此设计了DICE-PC和DICE-FT两种算法，在11个基准测试中分别平均提升4.3和8.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于最佳-of-N采样的单模型常常优于多智能体LLM系统，根本原因在于系统指定了智能体共享什么信息，但没有指定应选择哪种协调约定，导致均衡选择问题（ill-posed equilibrium selection）。

Method: 将多智能体系统形式化为折扣不完全信息马尔可夫博弈，提出异质性量化响应均衡（HQRE）——一种带智能体和状态依赖温度的熵正则化均衡概念；在单调性条件下HQRE唯一且镜像更新线性收敛；基于此设计了DICE-PC（通过提示控制协调冻结模型）和DICE-FT（参数高效镜像微调）。

Result: 在四个领域的11个基准测试中，DICE-PC平均提升4.3个百分点，DICE-FT平均提升8.5个百分点，在准确率-成本权衡上优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过引入HQRE解决了多智能体LLM系统中的均衡选择问题，提供了唯一的、学习稳定的目标，并基于此开发的算法在多个下游任务上取得了显著提升。

Abstract: Multi-agent large language model (LLM) systems often fail to reliably outperform a single strong model equipped with best-of-N sampling. We argue that a core source of this instability is ill-posed equilibrium selection: current systems specify what information agents share, but not which coordination convention should be selected. We formalize a broad class of such systems as discounted incomplete-information Markov games and show that two common pathologies, oscillation between competing conventions and drift across them, can both induce unstable learning and linear Bayesian regret. To obtain a well-posed target, we introduce the Heterogeneous Quantal Response Equilibrium (HQRE), an entropy-regularized equilibrium concept with agent- and state-dependent temperatures. Under a monotonicity condition, HQRE is unique, admits linearly convergent mirror updates, and yields bounded Bayesian regret; the same condition yields rollout-measurable stability diagnostics. We instantiate this objective in two algorithms: DICE-PC, which coordinates frozen models through prompt-control actions, and DICE-FT, which performs parameter-efficient mirror fine-tuning. Across eleven benchmarks in four domains, DICE improves accuracy-cost trade-offs over strong within-class baselines; on reasoning and planning tasks, DICE-PC improves by 4.3 percentage points on average and DICE-FT by 8.5 points.

</details>


### [114] [GPT-Micro: A large language paradigm for accelerated, inexpensive, and thermodynamics-consistent discovery of constitutive models in manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08238)
*Soumik Dutta,Kiarash Naghavi Khanghah,Sania Shree,Logan McNeil,Thomas Feldhausen,Hongyi Xu,Rajiv Malhotra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GPT-Micro范式，一种融合大语言模型、热力学守恒定律和稀疏数据集的自主本构模型发现方法，在印刷电子工艺测试中实现70%以上数据需求降低、400倍发现时间缩短，并生成符合物理规律的可解释分析模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统本构模型发现依赖人工经验和直觉，导致增量式、耗时的模型探索；常规机器学习需要大量数据生成，成本高昂；现有LLM模型发现方法忽略热力学基本定律的不可违背性。

Method: GPT-Micro框架整合了三方面：文献语义知识提取、基于热力学的守恒定律约束、稀疏数据集，并结合LLM驱动的模型假设生成与迭代优化，实现自主的本构模型发现。

Result: 在印刷电子工艺测试中，相比ML方法降低70%以上数据需求且精度无损失；相比人工建模将发现时间从数月缩短至数小时（400倍加速）；发现具有新颖函数形式的模型，无需主观选择初始假设。

Conclusion: GPT-Micro能够实现快速、低成本、物理可信且可解释的微观结构建模，在制造领域具有广泛应用潜力。

Abstract: Constitutive modeling of the relationship between process-imposed material states and fundamental material properties is critical to control of material microstructure in manufacturing processes. The limited accuracy resulting from the typical reliance on fallible human expertise and intuition for postulation and revision of the models functional form results in incremental and time consuming model discovery. Conventional Machine Learning (ML) incurs significant cost and time of data generation. Model discovery using Large Language Models (LLMs) suffers from the above issues and/or ignores the inviolability of fundamental thermodynamics laws. This work creates a novel GPT-Micro paradigm for autonomous, data sparse, and thermodynamics-compliant discovery of de-novo constitutive models. This framework seamlessly integrates semantic knowledge extraction from literature, enforcement of thermodynamics-based conservation laws, and sparse datasets, with LLM-driven generation and refinement of model hypotheses. Validation is performed for a long-intractable constitutive modeling problem in a printed electronics process testbed. This reveals significant and simultaneous advantages over the state-of-the-art including: (a) More than 70 percent reduction in data burden relative to ML-based modeling without loss in accuracy; (b) 400X reduction in discovery time after data generation, from months to hours, relative to human-driven modeling; (c) Discovery of models with novel functional forms without subjective human choice of a starting hypothesis; (d) Enhanced physics-rooted trustworthiness, human interpretability, and mechanistic insight via synthesis of compact, conservation-compliant, and physically complete analytical models. The potential of GPT-Micro to realize rapid, low-cost, physically trustworthy, and interpretable microstructure modeling across the manufacturing landscape is discussed.

</details>


### [115] [Causal Semantic Alignment for LLM-based Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08262)
*Kexuan Zhang,Xiaobei Zou,Cesare Alippi,Gary G. Yen,Yang Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CVAformer，一种针对LLM时间序列预测中变量异质性问题的变量级对齐框架，通过因果干预解耦动态成分与不变成分，并采用非因果注意力机制捕获变量间交互，在多种预测设置下取得SOTA或更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM时序预测方法忽视时间序列的异质性——动态波动与不变语义纠缠在一起，导致对齐过程中动态成分作为混杂因子引入虚假相关，影响对齐质量。

Method: 提出CVAformer框架：(1) 在对齐前将每个变量显式解耦为不变成分和动态成分；(2) 应用因果干预消除动态成分的混杂效应；(3) 用非因果注意力机制替代标准因果注意力，以捕获每个时间步上变量间的交互。

Result: 在长期、短期、少样本和零样本四种预测场景下，CVAformer在多数数据集上匹配或超越SOTA性能，部分数据集取得显著更优精度。

Conclusion: 实验验证了变量级对齐和动态解耦在CVAformer中的有效性，为基于LLM的时间序列任务提供了新视角。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have opened new possibilities for time series forecasting by enabling alignment between temporal patterns and pretrained word embeddings. However, most LLM-based methods overlook the heterogeneous nature of time series, where dynamic fluctuations and invariant semantics are entangled. This entanglement introduces spurious correlations during the alignment, as dynamic components act as confounders by simultaneously influencing invariant components and the resulting aligned embeddings. To address this issue, a variable-level alignment framework CVAformer is proposed. CVAformer explicitly disentangles each variable into invariant and dynamic components just before alignment, and applies causal intervention to mitigate the confounding effect of the dynamics. To better support variable-level alignment, CVAformer replaces the standard causal attention in LLMs with a non-causal attention mechanism that captures interactions among variables at each time step. Extensive experiments across long-term, short-term, few-shot, and zero-shot forecasting settings indicate that CVAformer matches or exceeds state-of-the-art performance on most datasets, and in some cases achieves notably better accuracy. Experimental results validate the effectiveness of variable-level alignment and dynamic disentanglement in CVAformer, offering a new perspective for LLM-based time series tasks.

</details>


### [116] [Causal Agent Replay: Counterfactual Attribution for LLM-Agent Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08275)
*Jaineet Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出因果代理重放（CAR）方法，通过干预式因果建模定位LLM智能体失败的根本步骤，而非仅观测结果或归因相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具只能回答智能体失败时"发生了什么"（可观测性）或"是否通过"（评估），但无法定位导致失败的因果步骤。直观启发式方法错误：执行有害行为的步骤通常不是决策步骤，LLM评判的归因存在相关性且不可靠（Who&When基准上最先进的步骤级准确率仅约14%）。

Method: 将智能体运行建模为结构因果模型，对步骤执行do操作后在前向随机策略下重新执行轨迹，测量结果分布偏移。定义了干预代数、单步对比估计器（含"承诺点"规则消除随机前向运行的混杂）、预算受限的蒙特卡洛Shapley估计器（拆分交互步骤的贡献），所有效应附带置信区间。

Result: 在合成结构因果模型上验证：对比估计器能恢复关键步骤，Shapley估计器能恢复两步交互（贡献0.44、0.45和~0，效率总和0.909 vs 解析值0.91）。

Conclusion: CAR通过因果干预而非相关性分析，有效且可靠地定位LLM智能体失败的根本步骤，开源并支持托管或本地模型运行。

Abstract: When an LLM agent fails -- issues a refund it should not have, calls the wrong tool, leaks data -- existing tooling answers what happened (observability) or whether it passed (evaluation), but not which step caused the failure. The obvious heuristics are wrong: the step that executes the harmful action is usually not the step that decided on it, and LLM-judge attribution is correlational and unreliable (state-of-the-art step-level accuracy on the Who&When benchmark is about 14%). We present Causal Agent Replay (CAR), which answers the question by intervention: it models an agent run as a structural causal model, applies a do-operation to a step, and re-executes the trajectory forward under the same stochastic policy, measuring the shift in the outcome distribution. We define an intervention algebra over agent steps, a single-step contrastive estimator whose point-of-commitment rule resolves a confound specific to stochastic run-forward, and a budget-bounded Monte-Carlo Shapley estimator that splits credit across interacting steps. Every effect is reported with confidence intervals. We validate against synthetic structural causal models with planted ground truth: the contrastive estimator recovers the pivotal step, and Shapley recovers a two-step interaction (0.44, 0.45, ~0; efficiency sum 0.909 versus the analytic 0.91). CAR is open source and runs on hosted or free local models.

</details>


### [117] [QueryWeaver: Reliable Multi-Tool Query Execution Planning via LLM-Based Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08300)
*Aishwarya Chakravarthy,Vidhi Kulkarni,Duen Horng Chau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将自然语言查询转换为结构化图，并通过确定性规划器（深度优先搜索）执行多步骤跨工具查询的系统，即使在小型本地LLM上也能实现高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在推理和工具使用方面表现强大，但可靠地执行多步骤、跨工具查询仍然困难，因为单个工具只暴露部分信息，而真实世界的个人数据查询往往跨越多个应用，需要结构化规划。

Method: 将自然语言查询转换为结构化图，通过确定性规划器（深度优先搜索）解析依赖关系并跨工具组合结果来执行查询。

Result: 即使在小型或本地托管的LLM上也实现了高准确率，支持超越传统关键词搜索的查询。

Conclusion: 该方法通过结构化图和确定性规划提高了多步骤跨工具查询的可靠性，并降低了对大型LLM的依赖。

Abstract: Many real-world queries over personal data span multiple applications and require structured planning, as individual tools expose only partial information. While LLMs show strong reasoning and tool use, reliably executing multi-step, cross-tool queries remains challenging. We introduce a system that converts natural language queries into structured graphs and executes them via a deterministic planner. Our approach uses depth-first search to resolve dependencies and combine results across tools, improving reliability and enabling queries beyond traditional keyword-based search. We demonstrate high accuracy even with smaller or locally hosted LLMs.

</details>


### [118] [Not Just After One: Sleep-Inspired Replay Prevents Catastrophic Forgetting After Sequential Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08447)
*Anthony Bazhenov,Jean Erik Delanois,Giri P. Krishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在连续学习场景中，神经网络可以依次训练多个新任务，然后通过类睡眠的离线回放阶段部分恢复所有先前学习任务的性能，模拟了生物学习中的"主动学习-睡眠巩固"模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服人工神经网络缺乏持续学习能力的局限性，即训练新任务时对旧任务产生干扰和遗忘的问题，模仿人类和动物在主动学习后通过睡眠巩固记忆的机制。

Method: 序列训练多个新任务，然后引入无监督的类睡眠回放阶段，通过离线重放来部分恢复所有先前学习任务的性能。

Result: 多个新任务可以顺序训练后再进行睡眠样回放，能够部分恢复跨所有先前学习任务的性能；任务特定信息对新训练保持弹性但随新任务训练逐渐衰减。

Conclusion: 发现了开发广泛连续学习AI解决方案的新原则，即利用类似睡眠的离线回放机制来缓解灾难性遗忘。

Abstract: One of the critical limitations of artificial neural networks is their lack of ability to continually learn: training on new tasks often leads to interference and forgetting of the previous ones. While several algorithms have been proposed to protect old memories from interference, they are typically applied during or immediately after each new episode of training. In contrast, humans and animals can learn continuously, acquiring multiple new memories during active learning before consolidating all of them into long-term storage. Here we show that multiple new tasks can be trained sequentially before an unsupervised sleep-like replay phase is applied to partially restore performance across all previously learned tasks. Our study further suggests that task-specific information remains resilient to new training but decays gradually as network is trained on new tasks. These findings point to novel principles for developing a broad range of continual learning AI solutions.

</details>


### [119] [Beyond Linear Activation Steering: Invertible Latent Transformations for Controlling LLM Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08454)
*Tuc Nguyen,Thai Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出INNSteer框架，通过可逆神经网络将LLM激活映射到潜在空间进行非线性控制，比传统线性干预方法更有效且保留生成流畅性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活操控方法将行为控制视为全局线性加性偏移，忽略了激活空间中行为特征可能呈非线性变化或位于弯曲各向异性流形上，导致最优干预依赖于输入内容。

Method: 学习一个轻量级可逆神经网络φ，将LLM激活映射到潜在空间（该空间中行为类别更易线性分离）；推理时通过φ映射、潜在空间操控、φ⁻¹逆映射回原始空间，实现原始空间中非线性、输入依赖的干预。

Result: 在多种LLM家族、规模、行为特质和安全基准上，INNSteer在模型控制效果上一致优于线性、传输基和非线性操控基线，同时基本保持生成流畅性。

Conclusion: 非线性激活操控（通过可逆潜在变换）比固定线性偏移方法更有效，为LLM行为控制提供了更灵活的推断时干预机制。

Abstract: Activation steering provides a lightweight inference-time mechanism for controlling large language models (LLMs) by modifying their internal activation vectors toward desired behaviors. Most existing methods compute a fixed steering direction in the original activation space, typically from pairs of contrastive examples using mean differences, linear probes, or arbitrary separability criteria. While effective to a certain extent, these methods treat behavioral control as a global, linear, additive offset: the same direction is applied across inputs, and behaviors are linearly separable. This can be restrictive when behavioral features vary nonlinearly across the activation space or lie on curved and anisotropic manifolds, where the optimal intervention may be input-dependent. To address this limitation, we propose INNSteer, a nonlinear activation steering framework based on invertible latent transformations. Rather than searching for a better steering vector in the original representation space, INNSteer learns a lightweight invertible neural network $φ$ that maps an LLM's activations into a latent space where behavioral classes are more amenable to linear control. At inference time, activations are mapped through $φ$, steered in the latent space, and mapped back through the exact inverse transformation $φ^{-1}$. This makes a simple latent-space translation become a nonlinear, input-dependent intervention in the original activation space. Across experiment settings on multiple LLM families, scales, behavioral traits, and safety benchmarks, INNSteer consistently improves model control over linear, transport-based, and nonlinear steering baselines while largely preserving generation fluency.

</details>


### [120] [PIPE-Cypher: Automatic Enterprise Benchmark Generation for Text-to-Cypher Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08481)
*Suraj Ranganath,Anish Raghavendra*

Main category: cs.LG
